Contract assets current 7,187 vs 5,005 at FY25; inventory 4,523 vs 2,270. Cash tax 2,228 vs provision 3,853, with global minimum tax payable 2,186. Growth consumes working capital and eventual cash tax can lag current CFO. [q2026](https://investors.broadcom.com/static-files/96641754-401f-4090-a4ab-210728c83a28)

[q2026](https://investors.broadcom.com/static-files/96641754-401f-4090-a4ab-210728c83a28) Value: 179200 USD millions; currency USD; period instant to 2026-08-02.
- **backstop [fact; supported; observed 2026-08-02]:** AI-rack customer-financing arrangement: maximum potential undiscounted Backstop ~$29B upon full deployment; liability on customer default is difference between 85% of outstanding lease Backstop and realizable rack value, with contractual remedies. Five-year leases; fair value currently immaterial and no payments. Not equivalent to zero economic risk or current funded debt. [q2026](https://investors.broadcom.com/static-files/96641754-401f-4090-a4ab-210728c83a28) Value: 29000 USD millions; currency USD; period instant to 2026-08-02.
- **convertible_commitment [fact; supported; observed 2026-08-02]:** Customer may under conditions issue Broadcom convertible promissory notes up to $42B solely for lease obligations. None issued August 2. This is potential financing exposure to a customer, not Broadcom convertible debt or free cash available to Broadcom. [q2026](https://investors.broadcom.com/static-files/96641754-401f-4090-a4ab-210728c83a28) Value: 42000 USD millions; currency USD; period instant to 2026-08-02.
- **purchase_commitments [fact; supported; observed 2026-08-02]:** Noncancelable purchase commitments (\>one-year remaining, primarily inventory), USD million: remaining FY26 1,110; FY27 52,674; FY28 72,952; FY29 85. These commitments accompany demand ramp but magnify cancellation/obsolescence risk; customer RPO does not establish one-for-one risk offset. [q2026](https://investors.broadcom.com/static-files/96641754-401f-4090-a4ab-210728c83a28)
- **concentration [fact; supported; observed 2026-08-02]:** Management estimates top five end customers ~50% of FY26 YTD sales; distributors account for 56%. Chip designs, foundry/supply concentration and customer-owned tooling create dependence on a small ecosystem; risks include customers insourcing, postponing orders, and capital constraints. [q2026](https://investors.broadcom.com/static-files/96641754-401f-4090-a4ab-210728c83a28)

## Coverage

- financials: partial. Five years/eight quarters of core flows, not a complete five-year balance-sheet panel. Pension/capitalized-software and latest leases not separately quantified. Current full dilution and economic guarantee loss remain uncertain.
- earnings: partial. Issuer release and 10-Q read; no verified full transcript or consensus. FY27/28 ambition available through secondary reporting only.
- macro: partial. Sep16 Treasury exact webpage publication time unknown; use Sep15 yield. No point-in-time inflation series reconstructed.
- news: partial. CNBC original inaccessible; Bloomberg full text paywalled, accessible lead only. No comprehensive litigation audit.
- prices: partial. Third-party closing prices, not official exchange certification. Complete 3/6/12-month returns and matched long-horizon benchmarks unavailable. Drawdown/volatility refer only to supplied July8–September15 sample.
- peers: missing. No verified comparable-company valuation multiples; peer-relative cheapness cannot be claimed.

## Methodology

Primary: supported dcf_fcff, nominal USD, financialScale=millions and shareScale=millions; per-share outputs are USD. Fair-value valuationDate=2026-09-16; horizon valuationDate=2027-09-16; cutoff/asOf=2026-09-16T21:43:41Z. Use an explicit ten-year consolidated operating forecast, with AI semiconductor, non-AI semiconductor and infrastructure-software build underneath consolidated revenue. Forecast operatingMargin means operating earnings after economic SBC and ordinary lease costs, before acquisition-related intangible amortization; it is an analyst normalization, not GAAP EBIT or issuer adjusted EBIT. FCFF = revenue\*operatingMargin - unlevered cashTaxes + tangible depreciation - capitalExpenditure - changeWorkingCapital. The last field contains operating receivable/inventory/payable investment; long-term customer financing and guarantee losses are separately valued in bridge.otherClaims as described by funding_exposure, not mislabeled as PP&E or ordinary working capital. Exclude cash interest and debt principal payments from FCFF, because debt is deducted once in the equity bridge. No SBC cashflow addback. Show reconciliation to GAAP and do not add acquisition amortization again in depreciation. Terminal steady-state NOPAT, reinvestment from growth/returnOnCapital, and growth below both returnOnCapital and discountRate are mandatory. Cross-check: separately saved supported pe valuations on normalized fully diluted annual earnings, after recurring SBC, interest and ordinary lease costs; normalize the discrete tax benefit and reconcile any acquisition-amortization adjustment with primary accounting. Never call a discretionary multiple an observed peer multiple. Use reverse-pe at each explicitly assumed cross-check multiple to solve earnings required at the current price and the common return hurdle; this is not a reverse DCF or analyst consensus. Report FCFF and P/E separately rather than mechanically averaging them. Sensitivity must include supported discountRates and terminalGrowths grids; operating uncertainty comes from independently justified revenue, margin, reinvestment and funding scenarios and separately saved stress inputs. No case probabilities or preferred verdict are established here. Operational provenance: one failed usage-limit attempt was reported by the coordinator before this resumed completion; this is not a financial correction round.

Broadcom is a profitable semiconductor/software operating company with substantial but changing cash generation. FCFF permits segment-led operating forecasts and an explicit debt/cash/contingency bridge, while current growth and financing commitments make simple capitalization of historical cash flow inadequate. Common observed base, all USD millions: TTM 2025-08-04 through 2026-08-02 revenue 89,104, GAAP operating income 42,814, acquisition amortization 7,994, GAAP net income 38,265, adjusted net income 48,345, CFO 40,653, capex 1,250 and SBC 8,482. Thus illustrative pre-acquisition-amortization EBIT retaining SBC is 50,808; this is not a forecast. Issuer FCF 39,403 and illustrative owner cash 30,921 are levered cross-checks, not FCFF. FY26 revenue 105,889 is YTD plus Q4 guidance arithmetic, not a reported result. Latest balance sheet is August 2, not September 16: cash 23,975 and principal debt 61,079; basic shares August 28 are 4,773.629865 million, distinct from diluted ownership. FCFE is supportable only after a separate normalized earnings/reinvestment/net-borrowing bridge; it is not the shared primary because the present task benefits from separating operating value and funding exposures. Bank excess-return/PB and REIT NAV/AFFO methods are unsuitable to this operating business. EV/revenue ignores very different segment margins and cash investment. Adjusted EV/EBITDA and adjusted P/E without SBC normalization overstate comparable economic earnings. A sum-of-parts would require reliable segment cashflow allocation and matching peers absent from this packet. No verified point-in-time consensus or peer valuation panel exists, so relative cheapness, measured beta, observed equity-risk premium and market-implied credit spreads cannot be asserted. Ordinary leases remain operating costs and are excluded from debt; their rent is not added back. Current lease/pension/capitalized-software detail, full dilution, customer financing terms/default/recovery economics and exact source clock times remain explicit limitations rather than assumed facts.

- Invalidation: Software retention fails under licensing pressure; Annual infrastructure-software revenue and pre-SBC segment operating margin; Revenue below34,000 or two quarters with segment operating margin below75%, against39,000/83% first-year assumptions. - Invalidation: Long-duration equity valuation no longer clears the return hurdle; Recomputed nominal WACC or sustainable terminal margin; WACC at or above12% or terminal economic margin below40% requires a new value calculation; a bullish operating narrative alone is insufficient. Customer funding is valued once and reconciled into horizon cash/remaining rights; gross stress is separate. 261 independent numerical checks and supported calculator reproductions passed. Remaining disclosed data/model uncertainties do not create a material accounting error. |
| Valuation methods and assumptions | 3 | 20% | Strong: FCFF is suitable, reproducible and supported by complete horizon/terminal/equity bridges, rate-growth and operating/funding/policy sensitivities, plus normalized P/E and reverse earnings hurdles. Long-run market scale/competitive durability and actual financing contract economics lack independent corroboration; wide FCFF/P/E spread is explained but not empirically resolved. These limit precision rather than invalidate explicitly conditional valuation. No unconditional entry is certified. |
| Counterevidence and independent cases | 4 | 15% | Excellent: dispatch records support separate fresh Astra/high case contexts, identical frozen evidence/framework and explicit sibling-read prohibitions. Both cases engage serious counterevidence, share economic definitions, and supply falsifiable thresholds. Synthesis adjudicates operating/valuation disputes rather than averaging targets, retains the optimistic multiple countercheck and distinguishes factual gaps from forecast disagreement. Shared-filesystem isolation limits are honestly recorded. |
| Horizon, catalysts and entry judgment | 4 | 10% | Excellent: current value, horizon ex-dividend value, dated dividend returns and min(return ceiling,MOS ceiling) are distinct and reproducible. Catalysts fit the twelve-month horizon, policy assumptions are sensitized, downside remains disclosed even at the entry ceiling, and actual entry requires financial evidence. No profit probability, short recommendation, exact contract valuation, guaranteed liquidity or personalized suitability is asserted. |
| Clarity and materiality | 3 | 5% | Strong: facts/assumptions and author judgment/audit quality are distinguished; materially different methods and substantive uncertainties are visible. Compressed number-unit spacing and dense technical prose reduce accessibility (C1), and frozen source narrative retains immaterial price rounding (E1). Both issues are bounded and do not change the financial conclusion. |

- **E1_event_return_rounding minor, open:** Frozen price_event/financial notes report Sep2-to-Sep3 return -2.75%; closes 367.24 and 357.16 imply -2.7447990415%, which rounds to -2.74%. Difference under 0.006 percentage points has no valuation consequence. Final author notes acknowledge the correct value, but original bound source narrative is unchanged. Fix: Use -2.74% in user-facing event-return prose. If updating frozen evidence/notes later, refresh dependent provenance and re-audit; no financial scenario change is needed solely for this rounding correction. Evidence: evidence.json, raw-financial-notes.md, audit-notes.md. 
- **C1_numeric_spacing minor, open:** Synthesis contains compressed number/unit phrases such as SBC7.5%, WACC11.676% and adjacent comma-number text. Calculations and units remain recoverable, but presentation is harder to scan than necessary. Evidence: synthesis.json.

FY24 includes discontinued EUC in consolidated net income/CFO, and SBC below is continuing expense. This affects comparability; current TTM has no discontinued operation adjustment.

## Accounting and cash-flow normalization

Q3 GAAP operating income 15,955 reconciles to adjusted 20,095 by adding amortization 2,006, SBC 2,019 and restructuring 115. Adjusted net income 16,372 equals GAAP 13,088 + 4,140 operating adjustments + 75 debt-extinguishment loss - 931 tax adjustments. Q3 GAAP diluted EPS 2.68 and adjusted 3.32 use different denominators (4,887 vs 4,937 million), so comparing the EPS gap as solely income adjustments is incorrect. Acquisition amortization is noncash but reflects acquired assets; SBC is a recurring economic expense. Expected FY27 intangible amortization is 6,980, so GAAP EPS can rise from runoff without equivalent cash improvement.

YTD CFO bridge reconciles exactly: 29,747 NI + 6,047 intangible/ROU amortization + 484 depreciation + 6,287 SBC - 1,051 deferred/noncash tax + 161 debt loss + 204 noncash interest + 31 other - 6,544 receivables - 2,253 inventory + 2,313 payables - 619 employee compensation - 2,893 other current + 1,036 other long-term = 32,950. Capex 1,013 exceeds depreciation 484; neither equals all investment obligations.

CFO includes nonrecourse factoring proceeds: receivables sold 3,950 YTD versus 5,651 year earlier. Current contract assets 7,187 versus 5,005 FY25 and inventory 4,523 versus 2,270 show growing working-capital commitment. Cash tax 2,228 trails provision 3,853; global-minimum-tax payable 2,186 creates future outflow. No automatic working-capital addback or assumption that cash tax remains low.

Illustrative CFO-capex-SBC economic owner-cash proxy = 30,921 TTM, not a forecast. It is levered (interest already paid). Discount it with a cost-of-equity framework and do not subtract net debt again, or explicitly unlever to enterprise cash flow before using WACC and debt bridge. Do not simultaneously subtract all SBC and impose identical full dilution cost without explaining double counting. Stock repurchases are financing cash flows and not capex.

Software revenue: Q3 3,465 upfront license revenue versus 1,916 prior year. Reclassification of these licenses to products has no total-revenue effect. Their timing means software growth is not synonymous with recurring subscription growth. Q3 segment operating margins of 61.3% semiconductor and 83.7% infrastructure software exclude SBC and amortization and should not be described as GAAP consolidated margin.

## Balance sheet, ownership, and commitments

August2 cash 23,975. Debt principal 61,079; carrying debt 59,419 after 1,660 discount/costs. Carrying balance reconciles 2,252 current + 57,167 long term. Principal net debt is 37,104, carrying net debt 35,444. Estimated fair value debt 55,990 is a different measure. Use one disclosed convention consistently. No material floating-rate balance identified: notes and term loans fixed, revolver and commercial paper unused. Debt maturities remaining FY26 2,252, FY27 zero, FY28 5,127, FY29 2,405, FY30 6,406, thereafter 44,889. $7.5B revolver is conditional availability, not cash.

Common shares August28 4,773.629865M; rounded August2 4,774M. Q3 GAAP weighted basic 4,766M and diluted 4,887M. Adjusted denominator 4,937M excludes assumed future SBC repurchase proceeds. ~4.94B can be an explicitly approximate conservative valuation denominator, not a verified current fully diluted cap table. FY26 YTD $8.450B program repurchases did not prevent outstanding basic shares growing ~33M from FY25. Split July12 2024 was 10-for-1; current figures already adjusted. FY21–23 original filing EPS/share counts need split conversion if used.

Ordinary operating leases FY25: liability 1,325, cash paid 277 already in CFO. Do not double count their expense in an FCF equity valuation. Latest quarterly lease update/pension funding/capitalized-software amounts have not been separately quantified, and remain explicit gaps.

Most important contingent exposure: AI-rack customer lease Backstop maximum ~$29B on full deployment, five-year terms, recovery formula 85% of outstanding obligations less rack sale value with remedies. Current fair value immaterial/no payments does not establish zero economic value. Customer may issue Broadcom up to $42B convertible notes under conditions for lease obligations; none issued. These are contingent customer-financing exposures, not Broadcom funded convertible debt. Avoid summing their full face values blindly because exposure relationships/overlap require clarification.

Unconditional purchases primarily inventory, \>one-year remaining: rest FY26 1,110, FY27 52,674, FY28 72,952, FY29 85. Significant ramp commitments shift the historic low-capex cash profile. Scenario modeling should consider funding/working capital and customer defaults; RPO $179.2B (~25% recognized next 12 months) is not proof of matched cancellation protection. Top five end customers ~50% of YTD revenue; distributors 56%. Customer concentration, foundry dependence, customer insourcing and price demands are material. New rack financing may change cash intensity even if reported PP&E capex remains low.

## Latest outlook, macro and subsequent events

Q3 reported AI $16.7B; Q4 management guides AI $21.7B and total $34.8B with adjusted operating margin 66%. FY26 total revenue 105.889B is simple YTD-plus-guidance arithmetic. Long-range FY27 AI $115B/FY28 $230B is available only via inspected secondary reporting citing inaccessible CNBC; analysts must independently label and haircut it, or omit it from the numeric base.

Verified supplement closing quote is $339.51 September16 20:00Z, Nasdaq USD. After-hours excluded. Split-only Close is distinct from Adj Close/total return. Sep2-to-Sep3 return -2.75%; Sep11-to-Sep14 -4.77%. Those observed changes do not demonstrate a unique cause or undervaluation. Full 3/6/12-month common-calendar market/sector comparisons were not established in this evidence stage. Parent price-metrics.json was consumed as a mechanical supplement: 49 closes July8–September15; August14–September15 AVGO -13.66956%, sample maximum/current drawdown -20.68683%, per-observation volatility 2.52787% (not annualized). Matched dates and split-only Close: SPY 776.34 to 757.39 (-2.44094%); SMH 587.82 to 542.11 (-7.77619%). Benchmark pages directly opened and parent raw/extracted data inspected. Dividends excluded consistently. All historical metrics end September15, with the separately verified September16 close used as the valuation quote.

Fed September16 18:00Z target increase 25bp to 3.75–4.00 preceded cutoff. Treasury nominal ten-year Sep15 5.00%; Sep16 5.01% has unverified exact webpage availability. Use prior day conservatively. Inference: higher yields raise required returns and customer financing costs; current fixed debt reduces immediate company interest sensitivity. Revenue ramp/funding risk may dominate near-term direct interest changes. Tariffs/export restrictions can affect supply and customer markets; filing says no material tariff impact yet, so do not invent current damage.

Post-earnings search found September14/15 AI slowdown debate and CEO reaffirmation (secondary source, CNBC inaccessible) plus September11 EU VMware licensing information requests (Bloomberg accessible lead, remainder paywalled). No final legal ruling or quantified penalty verified. These challenge ramp durability and software pricing/retention, respectively; financial impact unknown. No verified consensus or peer forward valuation panel. Coverage therefore marked partial, peers missing, not falsely complete.

## Forecast rationale and accounting detail
100B first-year AI assumes growth beyond Q4 86.8B annualized; 155B second year needs continued deployments but not the secondary 230B fiscal ambition. Software 36B normalizes upfront timing; nonAI 17B modest recovery. AI segment margin starts 61%, close to latest blended semiconductor margin, fades to51%; software82% to79%, SBC7.5% to6.5%, central1.5%. This supports 55.72% first-year economic margin, not averaging cases. Mature terminal46% and20% incremental ROC imply15% terminal NOPAT reinvestment. Funding 30B notes with10% lifetime loss and disjoint7B residual guarantees is conditional risk allowance, not observed terms.
Base first-year revenue fiscal bridge: 46 remaining days of guided91-day Q4, AI stub21700\*46/91; remaining319-day AI requirement and annualized rate are saved. Same bridge for total guidance34800. Segment allocation of the stub is approximate. Case-specific bull1.5-month interpolation is retained, bear46/91 interpolation retained; all reach the same September cash dates without relabeling FY27 as September2027. Prior-year September revenue is not observed. Base growth comparisons can use the explicitly synthetic guided97404.274725 denominator only; no historical observed growth claim is made.
Base tax18% cash versus19% normalized accrual in first year is intentional: temporary deductions affect effective operating cash tax; ordinary interest is assumed deductible at19%. Interest3350 after19% shield=2713.5. Using18% shield would lower horizon cash33.5, a small modeling gap, not an omitted debt claim. Debt cash interest is distinct from marginal cost6.2% in WACC. Future interest income on excess cash is not separately capitalized in FCFF; excess cash is added at face value. Customer-note interest appears net of financing taxes only in contract PV, never again in EPS or operating taxes.
Base notes30B, 10% lifetime loss, principal27B over years3..8. Interest receipts in schedule are net after22% financing tax; gross=net/.78, tax=gross-net. Residual guarantee tranche7B is assumed disjoint; gross3400 settlements correspond to4000 defaulted residual face at85%, with1000 rack recovery later. These are conditional cohorts, not known contractual allocation. Bull interest is explicitly after21%; bear listed receipts interpreted after23% to preserve author arithmetic. Bear-interest-is-gross alternative is calculated. Note principal+writeoff exactly exhausts all funded principal in each scenario. Five-year Backstop runoff does not establish note maturity; note dates are assumptions.
WACC derivation: bull .97\*(.05+1.2\*.045)+.03\*.06\*(1-.21)=.102302. Base .97\*(.05+1.45\*.0475)+.03\*.062\*(1-.22)=.11675955. Bear .97\*(.05+1.7\*.05)+.03\*.0625\*(1-.23)=.13239375. Risk loading is not a measured beta, and expected contract losses are not silently charged twice through a separate WACC premium. Target financing weights are approximate long-run valuation inputs, not a promise to borrow a fixed fraction of cash need.

## Horizon cash and funding bridge
cash_H=23975+FCFF1-afterTaxInterest1-2252-12844-netCustomerFunding1. No buybacks, acquisitions or new shares/debt in main year1. Debt_H=61079-2252=58827. Future SBC remains economically settled and is not added back to cash. Horizon excess cash subtracts each reserve; funding PV at horizon excludes first-year cash but includes remaining collection rights.
\| Case \| FCFF1 \| After-tax interest \| Net funding1 \| Cash H \| Reserve H \| Funding PV today \| Remaining PV outflows H \|
\|---\|---:\|---:\|---:\|---:\|---:\|---:\|---:\|
\| bear \| 44417.000 \| 2687.476 \| 17750.000 \| 32858.524 \| 15000 \| 10533.619 \| -6163.019 \|
\| base \| 49883.000 \| 2713.500 \| 12000.000 \| 44048.500 \| 15000 \| 8849.459 \| -2088.606 \|
\| bull \| 61212.400 \| 2739.000 \| 6000.000 \| 61352.400 \| 18000 \| 3180.106 \| -2489.163 \|
Negative remaining outflow PV is an asset, not a negative claim. Note outstanding is a diagnostic face amount, not an added second asset. The same dates/rates reconcile PV_today=(net_year1+PV_H)/(1+fundingRate). Annual liquidity schedule pays all debt maturities without refinancing as a stress capacity test; beyond-horizon liability choices do not alter the current/horizon bridge. Bull author expected later refinancing; the no-refinancing supplemental liquidity schedule is deliberately more demanding and labeled liquidity-only. Disclosed fiscal maturity buckets are approximated to September intervals; thereafter44889 is spread across last5 years solely for stress and sums exactly to principal61079.

## Source-time convention
Calculator source availableAt is required. q3_2026 uses2026-09-03T23:59:59Z after verified September2 publication date; q2026 uses2026-09-11T23:59:59Z after verified September10 filing. q4_2025/k2025 similarly use following-day23:59:59Z. These conservative bounds are not exact publication timestamps; evidence publishedAt null stays unchanged. Treasury September15 5.00% uses assumed prior-day-known bound September16 00:00Z; uncertainty is explicit. Price observedAt/availableAt September16 20:00Z is regular-session close observation, not verified webpage release clock. Only these directly needed sources appear in numeric calculator provenance. No post-cutoff retrieval time is misrepresented as pre-cutoff publication.

## Deterministic outputs and sensitivity
All per-share valuation, return, entry and reverse-EPS figures shown in synthesis come from supported CLI outputs. build-synthesis.cjs computes only operating/financing bridges and PV of unsupported standalone financing schedules; it does not replace the primary calculator. calculate-synthesis-checks.cjs invokes the same scripts/analyst.ts CLI through bundled tsx, preserving exact input/output files and command receipts. Current core command: npm run analyst -- calculate --input reports/AVGO/20260916T214341Z/valuation-input.json --out reports/AVGO/20260916T214341Z/valuation.json. Separate pe-input/pe-valuation; reverse-pe-\[14,16,18,20,22,24\]; policy-r\*-mos\*; shares-\[4800,5100\]; other-claims-\[0,3000\]; operating-stress; funding-zero-recovery; funding-bear-interest-is-gross . All retained calculate/reverse-pe command results completed successfully. A proposed hypothetical-entry quote was rejected because the tool requires observed marketPrice; no invented observed quote or unsupported return output was substituted. First core attempt was rejected because bear selected WACC exceeded grid maximum; added15% upper probe to both-date grid, reran successfully. Two initial schema-inspection JavaScript expressions failed (syntax/anyOf-versus-oneOf); no artifact mutation or financial result came from those attempts.
Funding-sensitivity.json saves risk-yield8/10/12/14%, lifetime note-loss0/10/30%, rack recoveries0/1/2x tests, holding other terms fixed. These are standalone contract PV probes. Full zero-recovery supported equity calculations show financing-only value loss is modest; acceleration plus demand/cash shock can be much more severe for liquidity. Operating stress is one year only, explicitly no extrapolated recovery delay; persistent stress is represented by the full bear path. No false precision or probability can be inferred from the grid.

Segment sums, EBIT margins, WC components, funding principal reconciliation, cash/debt bridge and supported CLI calculations were checked. Rejected hypothetical entry input is retained as a failed attempt only, not an executable completed valuation. All prices are USD/share; financials/shares both millions. Preserved bull/bear arithmetic matches official calculator aside from negligible binary rounding. Raw filing accuracy is inherited from verified evidence, not independently re-audited here. Contract and current cap-table gaps remain substantive. Notes and extra files are reproducible, but only parent independent review/audit can establish final completion. Quality is not investment confidence.

## audit notes

# Independent AVGO research audit

Evaluator: gpt-6-astra, high reasoning, fresh context \`/root/evaluator\`. Cutoff remains 2026-09-16T21:43:41Z. This evaluator authored none of the evidence, framework, cases, synthesis or valuation inputs. This final assessment incorporates \`audit-precheck.md\` and \`audit-calculation-precheck.md\`, which preserve exact prior coverage and calculations. Two operational usage resumes retained this independent context. No desired verdict or author self-score was supplied.

## Assessment and deliverable status

No unresolved critical or major defect was established. The finished work supports a conditional research conclusion and reproducible scenario values, not a certified contract valuation, a precise forecast, an automatic buy at the entry ceiling or portfolio suitability. The synthesis explicitly requires improved evidence before treating its entry ceiling as actionable. Its unattractive-at-current-price judgment is financially defensible under the selected FCFF framework; the audit does not establish its probability of profit or empirical predictive accuracy. A reader can reasonably choose a different growth/discount-rate view without either view automatically failing this audit.

Fixed rubric scores: evidence 3/4, financial 4/4, valuation 3/4, adversarial 4/4, decision 4/4, clarity 3/4. Reasons and actionable findings are also in review.json. The CLI, not this prose, determines the final weighted score and gate. Honest unresolved contract terms and forecast uncertainty are not penalized as false facts. Evidence and valuation earn Strong rather than Excellent because independent market/contract corroboration remains incomplete, even though explicit scenario boundaries and sensitivities preserve the conclusion's conditional validity.

## Stage assessments

### Collection

Checked retrieval-packet.json, original issuer/SEC filings and releases, raw source metadata, market supplement, funding supplement and source availability conventions. Credential-based collection was incomplete and SEC direct requests failed; browsing and issuer copies supplied material statements and notes. The original \[Q3 filing\](https://www.sec.gov/Archives/edgar/data/1730168/000173016826000080/avgo-20260802.htm), retained plain-text copy and Q3 issuer release support the key base. Later retrieval was not silently equated with earlier publication. Exact publication clocks for several sources, complete long-term price panels, consensus, peers and original CNBC transcript remain unavailable and visibly bounded. Required action: retain these limitations; none blocks this conditional research deliverable.

### Evidence validation

Checked identity/security, latest cash/debt/shares, revenue and segment accounting, CFO reconciliation, SBC and acquired amortization, factoring/working capital, minimum tax payable, purchases/RPO and customer-financing passages against originals. Recomputed all eight TTM helper inputs using CLI; contiguous fiscal Q4 plus YTD scope is correct. Historical samples from FY23 original statements and FY25 release/filing agree with listed annual revenue/CFO, lease and acquisition context. This is risk-weighted source sampling, not a second full extraction of every historical cell or exhaustive litigation review.

Reopened \[StockAnalysis\](https://stockanalysis.com/stocks/avgo/history/) to verify the September 16 regular-session close and exclude later trading; the quote remains third-party. Reopened the \[Fed statement\](https://www.federalreserve.gov/newsevents/pressreleases/monetary20260916a.htm), \[Treasury table\](https://home.treasury.gov/resource-center/data-chart-center/interest-rates/TextView?type=daily_treasury_yield_curve&field_tdr_date_value=2026), \[Benzinga report\](https://www.benzinga.com/markets/tech/26/09/61783739/ai-fears-hammer-chip-stocks-but-broadcom-ceo-says-demand-isnt-cracking) and \[Bloomberg Law accessible lead\](https://news.bloomberglaw.com/tech-and-telecom-law/broadcoms-vmware-curbs-face-fresh-eu-antitrust-scrutiny). The interest-rate observations, secondary characterization of long-range ambitions and limited scrutiny claim are supported. Paywalled material and unavailable original interview were not inferred. Current webpage reopening does not prove a precise historical publication clock; dates, conservative conventions and uncertainty remain disclosed.

Minor E1 remains: September 2-to-3 endpoint return is -2.744799%, conventionally -2.74%, versus -2.75% in frozen evidence/financial notes. This affects neither model nor conclusion. The synthesis author acknowledged the correction in final calculation notes; frozen source text itself is unchanged, so the finding is open with partial mitigation rather than falsely marked fully resolved.

### Framework

Checked driver definitions and claim links, units, primary FCFF/secondary P-E rationale, economic SBC treatment, lease convention, ordinary versus financing working capital, unlevered tax, horizon rollforward, capital structure and entry policy. FCFF is appropriate to the operating company; levered owner cash is only a check. P/E is an assumed capitalization diagnostic, not observed peer evidence. The framework prevents double-counting debt, acquired amortization, customer loans and future SBC dilution. The common 12% return and 20% margin-of-safety rules are labeled policy assumptions and sensitized. No framework defect established.

### Bull

Read complete arguments/assumptions/catalysts/invalidation conditions and checked mapping into the supported calculator. The case derives upside from continuing AI growth and durable economics while retaining cash taxes, substantial reinvestment, compensation and funding losses. It acknowledges imperfect RPO coverage, software timing, customer concentration and terminal dependence. Its large decade-end market and assumed risk loading are judgments, not verified external forecasts. The case itself concedes that current price fails its margin-of-safety rule. No unresolved material defect; preserve conditional framing.

### Bear

Read the full case and checked supported inputs/results. It allows strong growth and positive central liquidity, explains disappointment through slower ramp, lower margins and cash intensity, and does not require imminent insolvency. Its counterevidence seriously recognizes demonstrated AI growth and existing cash generation. Customer notes and residual guarantees are assigned to assumed separate cohorts rather than added blindly. Note-interest gross/net ambiguity is explicitly resolved in synthesis with an alternate gross-interest sensitivity; the small valuation difference is disclosed. No material unsupported factual default claim was found.

### Calculations

Re-ran primary and P/E CLI calculations: outputs are byte-identical to author results. Independent \`raw/audit-calculations.cjs\` reproduces 261 checks saved in \`raw/audit-calculation-checks.json\`, maximum absolute floating-point delta 2.33e-10 in USD millions. Coverage includes every primary FCFF and terminal calculation, equity bridges/shares, cash and EV horizon roll, financing current/remaining PV, annual gross liquidity arithmetic, revenue components, returns/dated dividends/entry ceilings, six reverse-P/E results and every financing sensitivity row. The eight TTM CLI results and price metrics likewise match originals. Reviewed sensitivity-generation scripts and receipts; completed output paths preserve their model assumptions.

Economic normalization is internally consistent: SBC remains an expense; no duplicate full future dilution, acquired-amortization addback or debt deduction; leases stay in costs; minimum-tax payable is settled once; future financing interest appears only in contract value; first-year cash does not remain in horizon operating flows. Main funding PV and separate gross-liquidity stresses answer distinct questions. Current debt/cash are stale observations carried by explicit assumption. Annual cash endpoints and approximate maturity buckets do not establish intrayear survival. Those limitations are prominent rather than hidden.

### Synthesis

Inspected final synthesis hash d57cb5f90d084b8718ab107a2db7cb29466eac14f292a5fa3ae7e2f576938ad6, final calculation notes and their mapping. Narrative values agree with outputs; the base is constructed from segment economics rather than target averaging. It distinguishes current intrinsic value from ex-dividend horizon value, avoids probabilities, and connects monitoring thresholds to the horizon. It displays the large FCFF/P-E discrepancy and explicitly recognizes that financing PV alone cannot explain it. Zero-recovery and temporary operating shocks are accurately described as isolated sensitivities; the bear supplies the persistent adverse operating path. Source constraints and possible future improvements remain visible.

The long-run AI market size and durable competitive margins are not independently demonstrated, and there is no original contract term sheet or peer/consensus calibration. Those gaps limit precision and support a Strong valuation assessment, but are not hidden behind an unconditional entry. Minor C1: compressed phrases such as SBC7.5%, WACC11.676% and punctuation adjoining numbers reduce readability in the synthesis. Improve spacing/units at future presentation refresh; no recalculation is needed solely for this editorial issue.

### Orchestration and provenance

Checked final dispatch-log.json, workflow-record.md, worker declarations and matching artifact hashes. Recorded separate fresh Astra/high bull and bear contexts consumed the same frozen evidence/framework and had explicit prohibitions on sibling/prior opinions. Synthesis consumed completed cases; evaluator remained separate, staged its review, and did not read unfinished cases or synthesize the investment case. This supports logical orchestration independence but is not an operating-system sandbox or complete hidden-read trace. Actual model token usage/cost is unavailable, not zero. The shared funding-gap research did not resolve terms or introduce asymmetric constraints. Forwarding the evaluator's objective rounding correction did not supply a desired financial verdict or compromise case independence.

A provisional full-handoff/final-author race was caught before review hash binding. The parent retained the earlier workflow in raw/workflow-pre-final-author.md, refreshed final notes/dispatch and explicitly refroze the workflow. Final workflow exact-byte hash 2b098fd9692105db77771a67242c6a152213a4760135f0aa3e72d8009758abf1 will be independently rebound through CLI. No stale provisional synthesis is accepted as the audited artifact. This is a resolved pre-audit synchronization issue, not an uncorrected gate failure or fabricated audit correction round.

## Supplemental funding source decision

Retained the earlier independent determination: initial platform financing scale, multiyear draws and lender amortization add context but do not establish the customer-note tenor/coupon, cash-draw mechanism, conversion, collateral priority or legal overlap. They do not force 42 billion immediate cash funding, five-year customer-note recovery or a 71 billion additive loss cap. No mandatory evidence restart was justified by the supplement. The actual cases/synthesis preserve those facts as unknown, provide assumption grids, and maintain correlated liquidity stress. An actual contract inconsistent with the assumptions would require new research and revised dependent valuations.

## Gate scope and preservation

Evaluator-run structural validation returned valid true, no errors, with the disclosed coverage and unknown-publication warnings. Those warnings were substantively assessed above. Financial/case/calculator integrity checks passed; no injection, fabricated citation, post-cutoff fact disguised as contemporaneous data, or unresolved major/critical error was found. No author artifact was modified. Final review uses fresh canonical hashes plus exact-byte financial/workflow/audit-note hashes. Deterministic audit outcome is saved separately so these bound note bytes do not change after review. Rendering/import still belong to the coordinator after the gate; this audit does not certify a not-yet-rendered presentation.

Evaluator review construction initially referenced existing supplemental files directly in artifactRefs. The CLI resolver only permits named core artifacts and the three bound notes, so its first audit gate correctly returned needs-revision for review-reference schema errors, with no financial blockers. That result is preserved as raw/audit-initial-reference-errors.json. Review references were corrected to permitted core artifacts/audit-notes.md, which retains the full supplemental coverage above; scores, findings and authored inputs were unchanged. Final hash binding and audit were then rerun. This is an evaluator serialization repair, not a financial research correction round.
